Nghe Part 4: Lecture: kelp forests and sea otters · lưu đồ: How otters affect kelp forests
↓ Otters hunted almost to extinction for their ______ → fur
↓ Otter numbers fell; urchin numbers ______ → rose
↓ Large areas of kelp disappeared
↓ Where otters have returned, the kelp has often ______ → recovered
↓ Ecologists call the otter a ______ species → keystone
Lời

Good morning. Today I want to talk about one of the most productive habitats on the planet: kelp forests. Kelp is a type of large brown seaweed. It grows in cold, shallow water close to the coast, and in good conditions some species can grow up to half a metre in a single day. These underwater forests provide food and shelter for thousands of species, from tiny snails to fish and seals. Kelp forests also matter to humans. They protect coastlines by reducing the energy of waves during storms, and they support important fisheries. More recently, scientists have become interested in their role in storing carbon, although how much carbon kelp actually stores in the long term is still debated. To understand how these forests stay healthy, we need to look at a small animal: the sea urchin. Urchins feed on kelp, and when there are too many of them, they can destroy a forest, leaving what scientists call an urchin barren: bare rock with almost nothing growing on it. This is where sea otters come in. Otters eat urchins. An adult otter needs to eat about a quarter of its body weight every day, because it has no layer of fat to keep it warm. In the eighteenth and nineteenth centuries, otters were hunted almost to extinction for their fur. As otter numbers fell, urchin numbers rose, and large areas of kelp disappeared. Where otters have returned, the kelp has often recovered. Ecologists call an animal like this a keystone species: one whose effect on its environment is far greater than its numbers would suggest. Today, kelp faces new threats, particularly warmer water. In northern California, for example, a marine heatwave, combined with the loss of a starfish that also eats urchins, led to the disappearance of most of the region's kelp. Restoration projects now include removing urchins by hand, with the help of volunteer divers.

Task 1 hai biểu đồ: Water use in one country
The pie chart shows how water was used in one country in 2020, and the bar chart shows the average amount of water used per person each day in homes in 2000, 2010 and 2020. Summarise the information by selecting and reporting the main features, and make comparisons where relevant.
How water was used, 2020 (%): 2020: Agriculture 62, Industry 20, Homes 14, Other 4
Water used at home per person per day (litres): Litres: 2000 160, 2010 145, 2020 128
Bài mẫu

The pie chart shows the proportion of water used for different purposes in one country in 2020, while the bar chart illustrates how much water each person used at home per day in 2000, 2010 and 2020. Overall, agriculture was by far the largest user of water, and household consumption per person fell steadily over the twenty-year period. In 2020, farming accounted for 62% of all the water used, more than three times the share of industry, at 20%. Homes used 14% of the total, while other purposes made up only 4%. Turning to domestic use, the average person used 160 litres of water a day in 2000. This figure dropped to 145 litres in 2010 and fell further to 128 litres in 2020, a decrease of 32 litres, or around 20%, over the period. Despite this decline, homes still accounted for a relatively small share of the country's total water use.

The bikes are very popular with students, who use them to {3} to college. Some people were {4} that the bikes would be stolen, but this has not happened. Next year the town plans to open ten more stations and to {5} electric bikes for people who live on the hills. For more information, you can {6} the town council's website.

C1 · khuyết đoạn: Learning to sail at forty

I had always assumed that sailing was something you either grew up with or never did at all. My parents were not sailors, and the closest I had come to a boat was a ferry crossing on a family holiday. So when a friend suggested that we sign up for a beginners' course on the south coast, I laughed. A week later, I found myself filling in the application form. {1} That first soaking turned out to be the most useful thing that could have happened. Once you have been in the water and climbed back out, the worst is over, and the fear that had been making me stiff and clumsy simply disappeared. By the end of the day I was actually enjoying myself. {2} All that study paid off on the fourth day, when the wind picked up and Jen sent us out in pairs without her. Suddenly the diagrams made sense. My partner, the retired teacher, called out the commands while I handled the ropes, and for the first time we were sailing rather than simply being carried along. {3} Looking back, it is that moment, rather than the calm afternoons, that I remember most clearly. Panic would have been the natural reaction, but we had been taught what to do, and we did it. There is a particular kind of confidence that comes from discovering that your training holds when it is tested. {4} Forty, it turns out, is not too late to start something new. It may even be the ideal age: old enough to know that falling in is not the end of the world, and young enough to climb back into the boat.


A What I had not expected was how much there was to learn before we could go anywhere. Knots, the names of every rope, the rules about which boat gives way to which: the theory seemed endless, and in the evenings I sat in the hotel bar with a book of diagrams, testing myself like a schoolboy before an exam.
B The equipment, I soon discovered, was surprisingly expensive. A good waterproof jacket alone cost more than I had spent on clothes all year, and I spent several evenings comparing prices online before buying anything.
C I passed the course, though not with distinction. More importantly, I have kept going. Every month or so I hire a dinghy at the local lake, and next summer I plan to join a club that races on the coast.
D The course began on a grey Monday morning in April. There were six of us, ranging from a retired teacher to a nineteen-year-old student, and our instructor, a quiet woman called Jen, wasted no time. Within an hour we were out on the water in a small dinghy, and within two hours I had fallen in.
E Not everything went smoothly, of course. On the way back, we misjudged a turn and drifted towards a line of moored yachts. A man on one of them shouted something I am glad I could not hear, and it took both of us, pulling with all our strength, to bring the boat round in time.
→ (1) D · (2) A · (3) E · (4) C
“That first soaking” chỉ về việc vừa ngã xuống nước ở đoạn D (fallen in); D cũng giới thiệu Jen và người thầy giáo về hưu nhắc ở sau. · “All that study” nối với việc học lý thuyết, xem sơ đồ ở đoạn A. · “that moment” = lúc suýt đâm vào thuyền neo ở đoạn E. · Đoạn C (đã qua khoá học, vẫn tiếp tục) dẫn tới câu kết “not too late to start something new”.
C2 · khuyết đoạn: In praise of boredom

Few emotions have a worse reputation than boredom. We treat it as a problem to be solved, usually by reaching for a phone, and parents who once told restless children to go and find something to do now feel obliged to provide a constant stream of entertainment. Yet a growing body of research suggests that this restless state may serve a purpose. {1} Psychologists have proposed several explanations for this effect. The most persuasive is that boredom acts as a signal, telling us that our current activity is no longer rewarding and prompting the mind to search elsewhere. Seen in this light, it is less an emotion to be avoided than an alarm worth listening to. {2} None of this is to suggest that boredom is always benign. Chronic boredom, the kind that persists for months rather than minutes, has been linked to depression and risky behaviour, and in certain jobs it can be dangerous: a security guard or a pilot who loses concentration may cause real harm. {3} What follows from this for the rest of us is surprisingly modest. Nobody is advocating hours of enforced idleness. The suggestion is simply that we might occasionally resist the urge to fill every empty moment, and see where the mind goes when it is left to its own devices. {4}


A The distinction, then, is between boredom as a passing state and boredom as a condition. The former may be productive; the latter is a sign that something in a person's life needs to change, and it is unlikely to be cured by a better app.
B Alarms, however, are only useful if we respond to them in the right way. The difficulty with smartphones is not simply that they distract us, but that they silence the signal before it has done its work. A moment of tedium that might once have led to daydreaming, and from there to a new idea, is now ended within seconds.
C The word itself is relatively young. 'Boredom' does not seem to have been widely used in English until the nineteenth century, although the state it describes is presumably as old as humanity.
D For children in particular, this may be a skill worth learning early. A child who is never allowed to be bored never discovers what he or she might do about it, and the capacity to entertain oneself is one that tends to last a lifetime.
E The most widely cited evidence comes from experiments in which participants were first given a deliberately tedious task, such as copying numbers from a telephone directory, and then asked to think of as many uses as possible for a pair of plastic cups. Those who had endured the dull task came up with more original ideas than those who had gone straight to the creative exercise.
→ (1) E · (2) B · (3) A · (4) D
“this effect” cần một kết quả nghiên cứu: đoạn E (việc nhàm chán trước giúp nghĩ ra nhiều ý hơn). · “an alarm worth listening to” → đoạn B mở bằng “Alarms, however…”. · Sau đoạn nói buồn chán kéo dài có hại, đoạn A phân biệt trạng thái thoáng qua với tình trạng kéo dài; “What follows from this” chỉ về sự phân biệt đó. · Đoạn D (trẻ em) nối với “resist the urge to fill every empty moment” và kết bài.
Movers Nói · Find the differences

• In picture A, the cat is on the chair. In picture B, where is the cat? → In picture B, the cat is under the chair. (cần nghe ra: under)
• In picture A, there are two apples. How many apples are there in picture B? → In picture B, there are three apples. (cần nghe ra: 3 / three)
• In picture A, there's a dog. What's in picture B? → In picture B, there's a rabbit. (cần nghe ra: rabbit)
• In picture A, it's sunny. What's the weather like in picture B? → In picture B, it's raining. (cần nghe ra: rain / raining / rainy)
Movers Nói · The kite

These pictures tell a story. It's called 'The kite'. Look at picture one. Sally is in the park with her kite. It's a windy day. Now you tell the story.
• Look at picture two. What happens? → The kite is in the tree. Sally is sad. (cần nghe ra: tree)
• Look at picture three. What does the man do? → A man brings a ladder and gets the kite. (cần nghe ra: ladder)
• Look at picture four. How does Sally feel? → Sally is happy. She says thank you. (cần nghe ra: happy)

• 👕 👖 👟 🍕 Which one is different? Why? → The pizza, because the others are clothes. (cần nghe ra: pizza)

✗ I usually have dinner at seven. [rel]
✗ The client signed the contract. [rep]
✅ Yes, I'll meet you at the restaurant.

💼 TOEIC Nghe · Part 3 câu hàm ý, Part 4 quảng cáo (v139) (5)

Kiểm: câu hàm ý chỉ một cách hiểu hợp lý theo ngữ cảnh; quảng cáo giống kiểu đề thật; đáp án duy nhất; hội thoại 3 người: A nữ, B nam, C nữ thứ hai.

t3-26 Part 3
Lời

A: Hi, Daniel. The caterer just called. They can't deliver the lunch for tomorrow's board meeting until one o'clock. B: One o'clock? The meeting starts at eleven thirty, and the directors usually eat during the break at noon. A: I know. I asked them to come earlier, but they're short of drivers this week. B: Well, there's a sandwich shop right across the street. A: Good idea. I'll call them now and see if they can prepare something for twelve people.


• What problem does the woman mention? → A delivery will arrive late. (✗ A meeting has been canceled. · ✗ A caterer has raised its prices. · ✗ Some directors cannot attend.)
• What does the man imply when he says, "there's a sandwich shop right across the street"? → The speakers could order food nearby. (✗ The meeting should be moved. · ✗ He is going out for lunch. · ✗ The caterer is located nearby.)
• What will the woman do next? → Contact a shop (✗ Visit the caterer · ✗ Reschedule a meeting · ✗ Hire a driver)
t3-27 Part 3
Lời

A: Paul, I've finished the slides for Friday's presentation to Brightline Foods. Could you take a look before I send them to the client? B: Sure. Hmm, the sales figures on slide six are from last year. A: Oh, are they? I copied that chart from an old report. B: Brightline's new figures came out on Monday. They're in the shared folder. A: Thanks. I'll update the chart this afternoon and send everything tomorrow morning.


• What are the speakers mainly discussing? → A presentation for a client (✗ A new sales office · ✗ A food delivery · ✗ A training course)
• Why does the man say, "the sales figures on slide six are from last year"? → To point out a mistake (✗ To praise the woman's work · ✗ To ask for a copy of a report · ✗ To explain a delay)
• What will the woman do this afternoon? → Revise a chart (✗ Meet with a client · ✗ Print some slides · ✗ Send a report to Brightline)
t3-28 Part 3
Lời

A: Thanks for coming, Tom and Grace. The hotel just told me the garden won't be available for our team dinner on the fifteenth. They're repairing the fountain. B: That's a shame. We've already sent the invitations with the hotel's address. C: The hotel has a rooftop terrace, doesn't it? I went to a wedding there last year. A: Grace, that's perfect. I'll ask the manager if it's free that evening. B: And I'll e-mail everyone if anything changes.


• Why is the garden unavailable? → Repairs are being made. (✗ It is too small. · ✗ It is booked for a wedding. · ✗ The weather will be bad.)
• What does Grace imply when she says, "The hotel has a rooftop terrace, doesn't it"? → The dinner could be held in another part of the hotel. (✗ She would prefer a different hotel. · ✗ She has never visited the hotel. · ✗ The invitations need to be printed again.)
• What does the man offer to do? → Send an update to the guests (✗ Call the hotel manager · ✗ Repair a fountain · ✗ Print new invitations)
t4-26 Part 4 · ad
Lời

Is your office running out of space? Let Stackwell Storage help. We offer secure, climate-controlled storage units in three sizes, and we can pick up your boxes, furniture, and files on the same day you call. Our units are open seven days a week, and every customer receives an access card that works at any of our five locations in the city. This month only, businesses that sign a six-month contract get the first month free. Call us at 555-0148 or book a pick-up on our Web site today.


• What is being advertised? → A storage service (✗ An office cleaning company · ✗ A furniture store · ✗ A printing service)
• What is mentioned about the access card? → It can be used at several locations. (✗ It must be renewed every month. · ✗ It is only for large units. · ✗ It costs extra.)
• What is offered to some businesses this month? → A free month (✗ A larger unit · ✗ A free access card · ✗ A discount on furniture)
t4-27 Part 4 · ad
Lời

Thinking about learning a new language this year? At Pathway Language Center, our classes are small, with no more than eight students, so you'll speak from the very first lesson. We offer evening and weekend courses in Spanish, Japanese, and Korean, and all of our teachers are native speakers. Not sure which level is right for you? Our free online test takes just ten minutes. And here's the best part: you don't have to wait until the next term. New groups start every Monday. Visit pathwaylanguage.com to sign up.


• What is being advertised? → A language school (✗ A travel agency · ✗ A translation service · ✗ A bookstore)
• Why does the speaker say, "you don't have to wait until the next term"? → To emphasize that students can start soon (✗ To explain why the classes are small · ✗ To announce that a term has been canceled · ✗ To recommend an online test)
• According to the speaker, what can listeners do in ten minutes? → Find out their level (✗ Watch a sample lesson · ✗ Talk to a teacher · ✗ Buy a textbook)

💼 TOEIC Đọc · Part 5 từ hạn định, lượng từ (v139) (10)

Kiểm: chỉ một phương án đúng ngữ pháp và nghĩa; giải thích đúng.

------- employee must complete the online safety course before the end of the month.
Every · All · Most · Many
Every + danh từ đếm được số ít (employee); all / most / many cần danh từ số nhiều (employees).
The new printers have been installed on ------- floor of the building.
each · all · both · several
each + danh từ số ít (floor); all / both / several cần danh từ số nhiều (floors).
Only a ------- of the applicants had experience in international sales.
few · little · less · much
a few (of) + danh từ đếm được số nhiều (applicants); a little / much đi với danh từ không đếm được.
There is very ------- time left before the product launch, so the team is working late.
little · few · many · several
time (thời gian) không đếm được → little; few / many / several đi với danh từ đếm được.
Ms. Okafor has reviewed three of the ten contracts and will check the ------- tomorrow.
others · other · another · each other
the others = những cái còn lại (số nhiều, đứng một mình); the other + danh từ, hoặc chỉ MỘT cái còn lại.
If this model is out of stock, we can offer you ------- one at the same price.
another · other · others · the others
another + danh từ số ít (one); other + danh từ số nhiều; others / the others đứng một mình, không có danh từ theo sau.
------- of the participants found the workshop useful, according to the survey.
Most · Almost · Every · Mostly
Most of the + danh từ số nhiều; almost là trạng từ (almost all of), every không đi với of, mostly là trạng từ.
The ------- of customers who shop online has doubled over the past five years.
number · amount · deal · level
the number of + danh từ đếm được số nhiều (customers), động từ số ít (has); amount / a great deal of đi với danh từ không đếm được.
Visitors may join ------- tour, but not both, because they start at the same time.
either · both · all · every
either + danh từ số ít = một trong hai; 'but not both' cho biết chỉ có hai tour và chỉ được chọn một.
We received so ------- applications that the hiring committee needed an extra week to review them.
many · much · little · a lot
so many + danh từ đếm được số nhiều (applications); much / little đi với danh từ không đếm được; a lot cần of.

💼 TOEIC Đọc · Part 6 câu chèn (viết lại) + 4 đoạn mới (v139) (24)

Câu chèn: 4 câu CÙNG chủ đề đoạn, chỉ một câu nối được câu trước và câu sau (3 câu kia sai vì mạch: sai thời gian, ngược ý, nhắc điều chưa có). Đoạn mới có chỗ thì theo ngữ cảnh [tense] và từ nối giữa câu [conn]. Kiểm: không có câu thứ hai cũng hợp.

t6-memo Memo · câu chèn viết lại

Please be aware that the east parking garage will be closed from Monday, March 3, to Friday, March 7, while repairs are {1} to the upper levels. During this period, employees may park in the visitor lot on Grant Street. {2} Shuttle buses will run between the visitor lot and the main entrance every fifteen minutes from 7:00 A.M. to 7:00 P.M. We apologize for any {3} this may cause. If you have any questions, please contact the facilities office at extension 4410. Thank you for your {4}.


(2) Please display your staff permit on the dashboard. · The repairs were completed ahead of schedule last week. · Visitors should therefore park in the east garage instead. · The shuttle buses will be replaced by a newer model next year.
Câu chèn nối ý đỗ xe ở bãi khách (câu trước): dặn đặt thẻ nhân viên trên xe. "repairs were completed" sai thời gian (từ 3/3 mới sửa); "park in the east garage" ngược ý (bãi phía đông đóng cửa); "The shuttle buses" nhắc xe đưa đón trước khi câu sau giới thiệu.
t6-email E-mail · câu chèn viết lại

Dear Mr. Moore, Thank you for your recent order of office chairs from Brightline Furniture. Unfortunately, the model you selected is currently out of stock. We {1} to receive a new shipment in about two weeks. {2}, we can offer you a similar model, the Ergo Plus, at the same price. {3} Please let us know which option you {4} by replying to this e-mail. Sincerely, Lena Fischer, Customer Service


(3) It comes with an adjustable headrest and a five-year warranty. · Your new chairs were delivered to your office yesterday. · Unfortunately, the Ergo Plus is no longer available either. · We will therefore cancel your order unless you reply today.
"It" = mẫu Ergo Plus vừa nhắc: câu chèn tả thêm mẫu thay thế. Ghế đã giao ngược với hết hàng; Ergo Plus cũng hết hàng ngược với lời đề nghị; tự huỷ đơn ngược với câu sau mời khách chọn.
t6-ad Advertisement · câu chèn viết lại

Looking for a place to stay active this winter? Harbor View Fitness Center offers modern equipment, group classes and a heated indoor pool. New members who sign up before January 31 will receive their first month {1} charge. {2} Our certified trainers will create a personal plan based on your goals. Classes are held {3} morning and evening, so you can always find a time that suits your schedule. Visit our website to {4} a free tour.


(2) In addition, every new member gets a free fitness assessment. · The heated indoor pool will close for the winter. · However, group classes are no longer available. · These trainers have helped thousands of people reach their goals.
"In addition" thêm một quà nữa cho hội viên mới, nối ưu đãi tháng đầu với câu huấn luyện viên lập kế hoạch. Hồ bơi đóng mùa đông và hết lớp nhóm ngược với câu đầu; "These trainers" nhắc huấn luyện viên trước khi câu sau giới thiệu.
t6-letter Letter · câu chèn viết lại

On behalf of the Riverside Business Association, I am pleased to inform you that you have been selected as this year's Small Business Owner of the Year. The award {1} the outstanding contribution your bakery has made to our community. The award ceremony will take place on April 18 at the Riverside Hotel. {2} We would be honored if you could say a few words after receiving the award. Please confirm your {3} by April 5. We look forward to {4} you there. Sincerely, Tom Becker, President


(2) Dinner will be served at 7:00 P.M., followed by the presentation. · Unfortunately, the ceremony has been canceled this year. · The ceremony took place last month at the same hotel. · We regret that your bakery was not chosen this year.
Câu chèn nói thêm chương trình buổi lễ (bữa tối rồi trao giải), nối với câu sau mời phát biểu sau khi nhận giải. Ba câu kia ngược với thư: lễ bị huỷ, lễ đã diễn ra, tiệm không được chọn.
t6-notice Notice · câu chèn viết lại

Starting next month, the Central Library will {1} its opening hours on weekends. The library will now open at 9:00 A.M. on Saturdays and Sundays, two hours earlier than before. This change is in {2} to requests from many users who wish to study in the mornings. {3} The changes will not affect weekday hours. For more information, please speak to a member of staff or visit our website, where the full schedule is {4}.


(3) Closing times will remain the same. · Weekday opening hours will also start two hours earlier. · As a result, the library will now open later on Saturdays. · Many users have asked us to close the library in the mornings.
Câu chèn nói tiếp phần không đổi (giờ đóng cửa), dẫn sang câu sau "không đổi giờ ngày thường". Giờ ngày thường cũng sớm hơn ngược với câu sau; mở muộn hơn và người dùng muốn đóng buổi sáng ngược với các câu trước.
t6-press Press release · câu chèn viết lại

Solmar Foods announced on Tuesday that it will build a new processing plant in the city of Dalton. The facility, which is expected to cost $40 million, will be the company's {1} in the region. Construction will begin in May and should be completed {2} eighteen months. {3} Company president Maria Solano said that Dalton was chosen because of its {4} to major highways and its skilled workforce.


(3) Once open, the plant will employ about 300 people. · The plant has already been producing food for ten years. · Construction was finished last month, ahead of schedule. · As a result, the company has decided to cancel the project.
Câu chèn nói kết quả khi nhà máy xong (khoảng 300 việc làm), trước câu giải thích lý do chọn Dalton. Nhà máy đã chạy 10 năm, đã xây xong, huỷ dự án đều ngược với bản tin.
t6-policy E-mail · câu chèn viết lại

Effective July 1, employees traveling for business must book all flights and hotels through our new online travel portal. This change will help us {1} costs and keep better records of business trips. {2} Employees who book travel outside the portal will not be {3} for their expenses unless they have written approval from their department manager. A short guide to using the portal is attached to this e-mail. Please read it {4} before planning your next trip.


(2) The portal also offers discounted rates with several airlines. · Employees may therefore book flights through any Web site they like. · The portal was closed permanently on June 30. · As a result, business travel costs have doubled this year.
Câu chèn nói thêm lợi ích của cổng đặt vé ("also"), nối với ý giảm chi phí. Đặt ở trang nào cũng được ngược với quy định; cổng đóng cửa ngược với "từ 1/7"; chi phí đã tăng gấp đôi "do đó" không nối được vì chính sách chưa áp dụng.
t6-product Product information · câu chèn viết lại

Thank you for choosing the AquaPure Water Filter. To get the best results, replace the filter cartridge {1} three months. A light on the top of the unit will turn red when a replacement is needed. {2} Replacement cartridges are available from most supermarkets and from our website. Do not place the unit in direct sunlight, as this may {3} its performance. For questions about your filter, contact our customer support team, which is available seven days a {4}.


(2) Simply twist the old cartridge to remove it and insert the new one. · The light will turn green when the filter needs to be replaced. · The filter cartridge never needs to be replaced. · Replacement cartridges are not sold in stores.
Câu chèn hướng dẫn thay lõi ngay sau câu đèn báo cần thay, trước câu nói chỗ mua lõi. Đèn xanh, không bao giờ cần thay, không bán ở cửa hàng đều ngược với hướng dẫn.
t6-hire E-mail · câu chèn viết lại

Dear Ms. Lopez, Thank you for applying for the position of warehouse supervisor. We were very {1} with your experience and would like to invite you to an interview on Tuesday, May 12, at 10:00 A.M. {2} The interview will last about forty-five minutes. Please bring a copy of your driver's license and any {3} you have received. If this time is not convenient, please reply to this e-mail {4} Friday. Sincerely, Hiring Team


(2) You will meet the operations manager and two team leaders. · Unfortunately, we have decided not to offer you an interview. · Your interview with our team took place last Tuesday. · We have already filled the warehouse supervisor position.
Câu chèn nói ai sẽ phỏng vấn, nối với câu sau về thời lượng buổi phỏng vấn. Ba câu kia ngược với thư mời: không mời phỏng vấn, phỏng vấn đã diễn ra, đã tuyển xong.
t6-menu Notice · câu chèn viết lại

Starting next Monday, the staff cafeteria will offer a new menu {1} more vegetarian and low-salt dishes. These changes are based on the results of last month's employee survey. {2} In addition, the cafeteria will stay open until 3:00 P.M., one hour {3} than before, so that staff working late shifts can enjoy a hot meal. Prices will remain the same. We {4} your feedback on the new menu.


(2) More than 400 employees took part in the survey. · No employees responded to last month's survey. · The cafeteria will now close at 2:00 P.M., one hour earlier than before. · Most employees asked for fewer vegetarian dishes.
Câu chèn nói thêm về cuộc khảo sát vừa nhắc. Không ai trả lời khảo sát, quán đóng sớm hơn, nhân viên muốn ít món chay đều ngược với thông báo.
t6-update E-mail · câu chèn viết lại

Dear customer, Bluewave Internet will carry out maintenance on our network on Saturday, June 7, between 1:00 A.M. and 5:00 A.M. During this time, your internet service may be {1}. We have chosen these hours because very few customers are online. {2} After the work is complete, you may need to {3} your modem. Simply turn it off and on again. We apologize for any inconvenience and thank you for {4} Bluewave.


(2) The work will make your connection faster and more reliable. · The maintenance was completed successfully last Saturday. · Most of our customers are online at this time of night. · Your service will therefore not be affected at all.
Câu chèn nói lợi ích của việc bảo trì, trước câu dặn khởi động lại modem. Bảo trì đã xong ngược với "will carry out"; nhiều khách online ngược với câu trước; "không ảnh hưởng gì" ngược với "may be unavailable".
t6-art Article · câu chèn viết lại

Golden Crust Bakery, a small family business on Elm Street, is opening a second shop in the city center next month. Owner Hana Ito started the bakery {1} years ago with only one oven. {2} The new shop will be twice as large as the original one and will {3} a seating area for thirty customers. Ms. Ito says the expansion will create twelve new jobs. "We're grateful to our loyal customers," she said. "Without {4}, none of this would be possible."


(2) Today, its bread is sold in more than twenty local supermarkets. · The bakery still has only one small oven and no plans to grow. · Ms. Ito plans to close the Elm Street shop next month. · The new shop opened in the city center last year.
Câu chèn nối từ lúc mới mở (một lò) tới thành công hôm nay, trước câu tả tiệm mới. Vẫn một lò không định mở rộng, đóng tiệm Elm Street (đây là tiệm "thứ hai"), tiệm mới mở năm ngoái đều ngược với bài.
t6-policy2 Memo · câu chèn viết lại

Training videos explaining how to use the system are available on the company intranet. If you need further {4}, please contact Human Resources.


(3) This will help the finance team process payments more quickly. · Paper forms can still be used for trips abroad. · There is no deadline for uploading receipts. · Receipts should therefore be mailed to the finance team.
"This" = hạn nộp 10 ngày ở câu trước; câu chèn nói lý do, rồi câu sau nói nộp trễ có thể không được trả. Dùng giấy, không có hạn, gửi hoá đơn qua bưu điện đều ngược với quy định mới.

Câu chèn nói thêm về các lớp nhóm vừa nhắc (three studios for group classes). Phòng tập mở 10 năm ngược với "khai trương"; hồ bơi chưa mở, máy đã bán ngược với câu trước.

t6-volunteer E-mail · câu chèn viết lại
